1	// 矿车实体(原版 Minecart 语义简化):沿轨道(v_314)骑行,无轨重力下落,跳车脱离。
2	// 轨道跟随:检测身体中心下方/前方轨道格,水平保持 vy=0 贴轨,左右加速 0.15/上限 8。
3	import { Entity } from './Entity';
4	import { TILE, GRAVITY } from '../core/constants';
5	import type { GameHooks } from './types';
6	import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
7	import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
8	
9	export class Minecart extends Entity {
10	  w = 16; h = 16;
11	  vx = 0;
12	  vy = 0;
13	  life = 3600;
14	  dead = false;
15	  mounted = false;   // 玩家是否骑乘中(由 Game 挂载)
16	  riderX = 0; riderY = 0; // 骑乘时玩家渲染位置跟随车
17	
18	  private trackAt(game: GameHooks, tx: number, ty: number): boolean {
19	    const st = game.world.store;
20	    return !!(st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)]
21	      && st.type[st.idx(tx, ty)] === (game as unknown as { trackTile: number }).trackTile);
22	  }
23	
24	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
25	    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标)
26	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
27	    if (!ctx) return;
28	    // 车身近似:灰底+深轮(素材暂无独立 cart sprite,用主色块)
29	    ctx.fillStyle = '#7A6A50';
30	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y + 6, this.w, 8);
31	    ctx.fillStyle = '#3A3230';
32	    ctx.fillRect(this.x + 2, this.y + 12, 4, 4);
33	    ctx.fillRect(this.x + 10, this.y + 12, 4, 4);
34	  }
35	
36	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
37	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
38	    const st = game.world.store;
39	    // 轨道检测:身体中心所在格及下一格
40	    const cx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
41	    const cy = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
42	    const onTrack = this.trackAt(game, cx, cy) || this.trackAt(game, cx, cy + 1);
43	    if (onTrack) {
44	      // 贴轨:轨道中心线 = 格底
45	      const trackTop = (cy + 1) * TILE;
46	      this.y = trackTop - this.h + 2;
47	      this.vy = 0;
48	      // 无骑乘输入时轻微摩擦
49	      if (!this.mounted) this.vx *= 0.98;
50	    } else {
51	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, 8);
52	    }
53	    this.x += this.vx;
54	    this.y += this.vy;
55	    // 撞墙停止
56	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2 + Math.sign(this.vx) * this.w / 2) / TILE);
57	    if (st.isSolid(tx, Math.floor((this.y + this.h - 2) / TILE))) this.vx = 0;
58	    // 落地消失(非轨道地面)
59	    if (this.y > game.world.store.h * TILE - 32) this.dead = true;
60	  }
61	}
62	

```

1262		{

1273		public static void GetWheelsPositions(Player player, Vector2 Position, int Width, int Height, bool followDown, bool followUp, Mount.MountDelegatesData delegatesData, out Vector2 leftWheel, out Vector2 rightWheel)
1274		{

1276			rightWheel = Position;

970	  getDesiredDirectionFrom(offset: { x: number; y: number }): Pt {
971	    if (this.lockedDirection) return this.lockedDirection;
972	    let num = Math.atan2(offset.y, offset.x);
973	    if (num < 0) num += Math.PI * 2;
974	    const step = Math.PI / 4;
975	    const ang = Math.floor(((num + step / 2) % (Math.PI * 2)) / step) * step;
976	    // || 0 归一化 -0（cos(π/2) 浮点残尾 trunc 后会是 -0）
977	    return { x: Math.trunc(Math.cos(ang) * 1.5) || 0, y: Math.trunc(Math.sin(ang) * 1.5) || 0 };
978	  }

981	  private stepMinecartTracks(
982	    env: SmartCursorEnv, sem: HeldSemantics, reach: Region, screenX: number, screenY: number,
983	    set: (x: number, y: number) => void, taken: () => boolean,

990	      // :1170-1196 主方向判定（±0.5 阈值；斜向组合时水平让位）
991	      const dx = env.mouse.x - p.cx, dy = env.mouse.y - p.cy;
992	      const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
993	      const upDot = -dy / len;
994	      const up = upDot >= 0.5, down = upDot <= -0.5;
995	      const rightDot = dx / len;
996	      let right = rightDot >= 0.5, left = rightDot <= -0.5;
997	      const upLeft = up && left, upRight = up && right, downLeft = down && left, downRight = down && right;
998	      if (upLeft || downLeft) left = false;
999	      if (upRight || downRight) right = false;
1000	      // :1197-1201 鼠标格已在轨道上 → 不动
1001	      const hoverSheet = sheetOf(st.get(screenX, screenY));
1002	      if (hoverSheet !== 314) {
1003	        const targets: Pt[] = [];
1004	        for (let i = reach.x0; i <= reach.x1; i++) {
1005	          for (let j = reach.y0; j <= reach.y1; j++) {
1006	            if (!st.inBounds(i, j) || sheetOf(st.get(i, j)) !== 314) continue;
1007	            const diag = (ox: number, oy: number): boolean => st.inBounds(i + ox, j + oy) && sheetOf(st.get(i + ox, j + oy)) === 314;
1008	            const open = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
1009	              if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;

1012	            };
1013	            // :1215-1238 六方向延伸（斜向带让位规则）
1014	            if (upLeft && open(i - 1, j - 1) && !(!diag(1, 1) && diag(1, -1)) && !diag(-1, 1)) targets.push({ x: i - 1, y: j - 1 });
1015	            if (left && open(i - 1, j)) targets.push({ x: i - 1, y: j });
1016	            if (downLeft && open(i - 1, j + 1) && !(!diag(1, -1) && diag(1, 1)) && !diag(-1, -1)) targets.push({ x: i - 1, y: j + 1 });

1018	            if (right && open(i + 1, j)) targets.push({ x: i + 1, y: j });
1019	            if (downRight && open(i + 1, j + 1) && !(!diag(-1, -1) && diag(-1, 1)) && !diag(1, -1)) targets.push({ x: i + 1, y: j + 1 });

1024	          const above = st.inBounds(t.x, t.y - 1) && sheetOf(st.get(t.x, t.y - 1)) === 314;

1030	          set(point.x, point.y);

1032	        }
1033	      }

	private bool ModifyFlexibleWandPlacementInfo(ref int tileType, ref int tileStyle, ref int? forcedRandom)
	{
		FlexibleTileWand flexibleTileWand = HeldItem.GetFlexibleTileWand();
		if (flexibleTileWand == null)
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (whoAmI == Main.myPlayer)
		{
			Point point = new Point(tileTargetX, tileTargetY);
			if (FlexibleWandLastPosition != point)
			{
				FlexibleWandLastPosition = point;
				FlexibleWandRandomSeed = Main.rand.Next();
			}
		}
		if (flexibleTileWand.TryGetPlacementOption(this, FlexibleWandRandomSeed, FlexibleWandCycleOffset, out var option, out var _))
		{
			tileType = option.TileIdToPlace;
			tileStyle = option.TileStyleToPlace;
			forcedRandom = FlexibleWandCycleOffset;
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

26	  354: 5,  // 巫惑台（8 帧）
27	  355: 5,  // 炼金台
28	  377: 5,  // 磨刀站（4 帧，pitch 38）
29	  499: 5,  // 腐变室 Decay Chamber（8 帧）
30	};
31	
32	/** pitch 54 特例组（其余走默认 38，TileDrawing GetTileDrawData switch 实证） */
33	const PITCH_54 = new Set([106, 219, 220, 228, 231, 243, 247, 300, 301, 302, 303, 305, 306, 307, 308, 354, 355, 499, 592]);
34	
35	/** 篝火族（TileDrawing.cs:6124-6133 Campfires 覆盖）：pitch=36（非默认 38！素材帧块
36	 *  36px 无缝排布——曾按默认 38 导致每帧 +2px 漂移，火焰像胶片滚动）；
37	 *  frameY≥36 的行（熄灭/灰烬变体）恒取静止帧 252 */
38	export function campfireYOffset(frameY: number): number {
39	  return frameY < 36 ? animFrameIdx(215, 324) * 36 : 252;
40	}
41

43	export const tileAnim = { tick: 0 };
44	
45	/** 该 sheet 当前动画帧索引（无动画表项/单帧返回 0）。
46	 *  sheetHeightPx = 素材总像素高（rows×18）；pitch 54 特例组外默认 38 */
47	export function animFrameIdx(sheet: number, sheetHeightPx: number): number {
48	  const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
49	  if (!rate) return 0;
50	  const pitch = PITCH_54.has(sheet) ? 54 : 38;
51	  const frames = Math.floor(sheetHeightPx / pitch);
52	  if (frames <= 1) return 0;
53	  return Math.floor(tileAnim.tick / rate) % frames;
54	}
55	
56	/** 绘制时的 frameY 偏移（= 帧索引 × pitch） */
57	export function animYOffset(sheet: number, sheetHeightPx: number): number {
58	  const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
59	  if (!rate) return 0;
60	  const pitch = PITCH_54.has(sheet) ? 54 : 38;
61	  return animFrameIdx(sheet, sheetHeightPx) * pitch;
62	}

240	  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
241	  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
242	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
243	    if (!m) return null;
244	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
245	    if (!img) return null;
246	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
247	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };
248	  }
249	
250	  /** 原版 NPC 贴图表取帧（纵向帧条，frameIdx 0-based）。
251	   *  未登记的 id 懒加载 vanilla/NPC_{id}.png（帧数来自 vanilla-npcs.json），首帧返回 null 下一帧生效 */
252	  private lazyNpcMeta = new Map<string, VanillaNpcMeta>();
253	  /** ⚠仅适用【纵向帧条】NPC 表。横向变体横条 NPC（如 594 风气球 = 8 列×32px 变体,
254	   *  Main.cs:23383 Frame(8,1,ai[2])）走此路径会把整条横排画出来——此类 NPC 必须
255	   *  在 Renderer.drawEnemy 加专属分支按列切片（见 drawWindyBalloon）。 */
256	  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
257	    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
258	    if (!m) {
259	      const key = String(npcId);
260	      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
261	      if (!m) {
262	        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
263	        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
264	        if (!img) {
265	          const im = new Image();
266	          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
267	          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
268	          return null;
269	        }
270	        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
271	        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
272	        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
273	        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
274	        m = meta;
275	      }
276	    }
277	    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
278	    if (!img) return null;
279	    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
280	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };
281	  }
282	
283	  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
284	  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
285	  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
286	
287	  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
288	    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
289	  }
290	
291	  /** 原版杂项单图（呼吸气泡等） */
292	  vmisc(path: string): DrawRect | null {
293	    const img = this.vimages.get(path);
294	    if (!img) return null;
295	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
296	  }
297	
298	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
299	   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底) */
300	  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
301	    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
302	    if (!path) return null;
303	    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
304	    if (!img) return null;
305	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
306	  }
307	
308	  private _uiPending = new Set<string>();
309	  private ensureUiImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
310	    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
311	    if (hit) return hit;
312	    if (this._uiPending.has(file)) return null;
313	    this._uiPending.add(file);
314	    const im = new Image();
315	    im.onload = () => { this.uiimages.set(file, im); this._uiPending.delete(file); };
316	    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); };
317	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
318	    return null;
319	  }
320	
321	  /** 原版物品图标。构建期已 shelf-pack 进少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(ix/iy/iw/ih
322	   *  子矩形,见 scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs);旧单体条目(无矩形字段)回退整图语义。
323	   *  未加载时触发后台懒加载并返回 null(下帧生效) */
324	  vicon(itemId: number): DrawRect | null {
325	    const m = this.vanilla.items[String(itemId)];
326	    if (!m) return null;
327	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.icon);
328	    if (!img) return null;
329	    if (m.ix !== undefined && m.iw !== undefined && m.ih !== undefined) {
330	      return { img, sx: m.ix, sy: m.iy ?? 0, sw: m.iw, sh: m.ih };
331	    }
332	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
333	  }
334	
335	  private _iconPending = new Set<string>();
336	  private _worldPreloaded = false;
337	  /** 懒加载完成回调(Game 注册 → ChunkCache 全量标脏:晚到的表重新烘焙
338	   *  已缓存的 chunk,否则 fallback 会永久烤进 canvas) */
339	  onVImageLoaded: ((file: string) => void) | null = null;
340	
341	  /** 预载文件清单(去重+decode)。tile/wall/NPC/misc 表与图标的统一底层 */
342	  async preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
343	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
344	    let done = 0;
345	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
346	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
347	      const img = new Image();
348	      img.onload = () => {
349	        this.vimages.set(f, img);
350	        // decode():预载阶段完成解码,否则 Chrome 延迟到首次 draw 才解码,
351	        // 大表(2000px 级)首帧仍会卡一拍——"预载"语义要求字节+解码双就绪
352	        img.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
353	        this.onVImageLoaded?.(f);
354	      };
355	      img.onerror = () => resolve();
356	      img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
357	    })));
358	  }
359

400	  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {

405	    const im = new Image();
406	    im.onload = () => {
407	      this.vimages.set(file, im);
408	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
409	      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
410	    };
411	    im.onerror = () => { this._iconPending.delete(file); };
412	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;

429	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }

TEX_POS[DECOR_LEFT_DOWN] = [0, 6];\n  TEX_POS[DECOR_RIGHT_DOWN] = [1, 6];\n  TEX_POS[DECOR_REGULAR_BUMPER] = [0, 7];\n  TEX_POS[DECOR_BOUNCY_BUMPER] = [1, 7];\n  for (let i = 0; i < TEX_POS.length; i++) {\n    TEX_POS[i] = [TEX_POS[i][0] * 18, TEX_POS[i][1] * 18];\n  }\n  // 子段高度换算：(8-h)*2（Minecart.cs:437-447，负值保留特殊语义）\n  for (let i = 0; i < TRACK_FRAMES; i++) {\n    const arr = TILE_HEIGHT[i];\n    for (let s = 0; s < 8; s++) if (arr[s] >= 0) arr[s] = (8 - arr[s]) * 2;\n  }\n  // _trackSwitchOptions[64]（Minecart.cs:448-518）：邻接位掩码 → 可选帧列表\n  const array3 = new Int32Array(TRACK_FRAMES);\n  for (let mask = 0; mask < 64; mask++) {\n    let count = 0;\n    for (let bit = 1, b = 0; bit < 256; bit <<= 1, b++) if ((mask & bit) === bit) count++;\n    for (let f = 0; f < TRACK_FRAMES; f++) array3[f] = -1;\n    let total = 0;\n    for (let f = 0; f < TRACK_FRAMES; f++) {\n      let conn = 0;\n      switch (LEFT_CONN[f]) {\n        case TOP: conn |= 1; break;\n        case MIDDLE: conn |= 2; break;\n        case BOTTOM: conn |= 4; break;\n      }\n      switch (RIGHT_CONN[f]) {\n        case TOP: conn |= 8; break;\n        case MIDDLE: conn |= 0x10; break;\n        case BOTTOM: conn |= 0x20; break;\n      }\n      if (count < 2) { if (mask !== conn) continue; }\n      else if (conn === 0 || (mask & conn) !== conn) continue;\n      array3[f] = f;\n      total++;\n    }\n    if (total === 0) continue;\n    const opts = new Int32Array(total);\n    let n = 0;\n    for (let f = 0; f < TRACK_FRAMES; f++) if (array3[f] !== -1) opts[n++] = array3[f];\n    TRACK_SWITCH_OPTIONS[mask] = opts;\n  }\n  // 首帧索引（Minecart.cs:519-546）\n  FIRST_PRESSURE_FRAME = -1; FIRST_LEFT_BOOST_FRAME = -1; FIRST_RIGHT_BOOST_FRAME = -1;\n  for (let f = 0; f < TRACK_FRAMES; f++) {\n    switch (TRACK_TYPE[f]) {\n      case TYPE_PRESSURE:\n        if (FIRST_PRESSURE_FRAME === -1) FIRST_PRESSURE_FRAME = f;\n        break;\n      case TYPE_BOOSTER:\n        if (BOOST_LEFT[f]) { if (FIRST_LEFT_BOOST_FRAME === -1) FIRST_LEFT_BOOST_FRAME = f; }\n        else if (FIRST_RIGHT_BOOST_FRAME === -1) FIRST_RIGHT_BOOST_FRAME = f;\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n}\nbuildTables();\n\n// ============================================================================\n// 存取助手（frameX/frameY 的 Uint16 ↔ int16 语义）\n// ============================================================================\nexport function frontTrack(st: TileStore, i: number): number { return st.frameX[i]; }\nexport function backTrack(st: TileStore, i: number): number {\n  return st.frameY[i] === 0xFFFF ? -1 : st.frameY[i];\n}\n/** 该格是否为有效轨道格（nactive && type==314 && frameX 合法；Minecart.cs:633） */\nexport function isTrackCell(st: TileStore, i: number): boolean {\n  return TRACK_TILE >= 0 && !!(st.flags[i] & 1) && !(st.wire[i] & 32)   // nactive: active && !inActive\n    && st.type[i] === TRACK_TILE && st.frameX[i] < TRACK_FRAMES;\n}\nexport function trackTypeOf(frame: number): number {\n  return frame >= 0 && frame < TRACK_FRAMES ? TRACK_TYPE[frame] : -1;\n}\nexport function tileHeightAt(frame: number, seg: number): number {\n  return frame >= 0 && frame < TRACK_FRAMES ? TILE_HEIGHT[frame][seg] : 0;\n}\nexport function leftConnOf(frame: number): number {\n  return frame >= 0 && frame < TRACK_FRAMES ? LEFT_CONN[frame] : NO_CONNECTION;\n}\nexport function rightConnOf(frame: number): number {\n  return frame >= 0 && frame < TRACK_FRAMES ? RIGHT_CONN[frame] : NO_CONNECTION;\n}\n/** IsPressurePlate（Minecart.cs:549-560）：压板轨道帧 20/21 */\nexport function isPressureFrame(frameX: number): boolean {\n  return frameX === 20 || frameX === 21;\n}\n/** GetSourceRect（Minecart.cs:1525-1538）：帧 ID → 素材表像素位（16×16）。\n *  加速轨道（type 2）行随 boosterAnimFrame() 滚动；装饰帧 ID(36+) 忽略动画参数 */\nexport function sourceRectOf(frameID: number, animationFrame = 0): { sx: number; sy: number } | null {\n  if (frameID < 0 || frameID >= 40) return null;\n  const p = TEX_POS[frameID];\n  let sy = p[1];\n  if (frameID < TRACK_FRAMES && TRACK_TYPE[frameID] === TYPE_BOOSTER) sy += 18 * animationFrame;\n  return { sx: p[0], sy };\n}\n/** Main.cs:18734-18741：tileFrameCounter[314]>=10 推进、>4 回卷 → 5 帧循环 */\nexport function boosterAnimFrame(): number {\n  return Math.floor(tileAnim.tick / 10) % 5;\n}\n// DrawLeftDecoration / DrawRightDecoration（Minecart.cs:1448-1464）：下行装饰条件\nexport function drawLeftDecoration(frameID: number): boolean {\n  return frameID >= 0 && frameID < TRACK_FRAMES && LEFT_CONN[frameID] === BOTTOM;\n}\nexport function drawRightDecoration(frameID: number): boolean {\n  return frameID >= 0 && frameID < TRACK_FRAMES && RIGHT_CONN[frameID] === BOTTOM;\n}\n// DrawBumper / DrawBouncyBumper（Minecart.cs:1466-1490）：端挡板条件（上端开放才画）\nexport function drawBumper(frameID: number): boolean {\n  if (frameID < 0 || frameID >= TRACK_FRAMES) return false;\n  if (TILE_HEIGHT[frameID][0] !== BUMPER_END) return TILE_HEIGHT[frameID][7] === BUMPER_END;\n  return true;\n}\nexport function drawBouncyBumper(frameID: number): boolean {\n  if (frameID < 0 || frameID >= TRACK_FRAMES) return false;\n  if (TILE_HEIGHT[frameID][0] !== BOUNCY_END) return TILE_HEIGHT[frameID][7] === BOUNCY_END;\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// GetNearbyTilesSetLookupIndex（Minecart.cs:1174-1202）：六邻轨道位掩码\n// 位序：左上 1 / 左 2 / 左下 4 / 右上 8 / 右 16 / 右下 32\n// ============================================================================\nexport function nearbyMask(st: TileStore, i: number, j: number): number {\n  let mask = 0;\n  const at = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>\n    st.inBounds(x, y) && !!(st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] & 1) && st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === TRACK_TILE;\n  if (at(i - 1, j - 1)) mask++;\n  if (at(i - 1, j)) mask += 2;\n  if (at(i - 1, j + 1)) mask += 4;\n  if (at(i + 1, j - 1)) mask += 8;\n  if (at(i + 1, j)) mask += 16;\n  if (at(i + 1, j + 1)) mask += 32;\n  return mask;\n}\n\n/** 写回前后轨帧（唯一写入口：保持 type/active 不变，走 setTile 联动渲染/联机） */\nfunction writeFrames(st: TileStore, i: number, j: number, front: number, back: number): void {\n  const idx = st.idx(i, j);\n  st.setTile(i, j, st.type[idx], front, back < 0 ? 0xFFFF : back);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// FrameTrack（Minecart.cs:947-1172）\n//   pound=false：按邻接计算应取连接（放置/重 framing）\n//   pound=true ：锤击循环切换（Player.cs:45444）\n//   mute=true  ：仅轨道格响应（FlipSwitchTrack 对加速轨的翻向用）\n// 返回 false = 无法定帧（孤格置默认平轨 / pound 无可切换项）。\n// ============================================================================\nexport function frameTrack(st: TileStore, i: number, j: number, pound = false, mute = false): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return false;\n  const idx = st.idx(i, j);\n  if (!isTrackCell(st, idx)) {\n    if (mute) return false;\n    writeFrames(st, i, j, 0, -1);\n    return false;\n  }\n  const num = frontTrack(st, idx);          // 当前前轨\n  const num2 = backTrack(st, idx);          // 当前后轨\n  const num3 = trackTypeOf(num);            // 当前轨道类型\n  let num4 = -1;                            // array 下标（前轨）\n  let num5 = -1;                            // array 下标（后轨）\n  const array = TRACK_SWITCH_OPTIONS[nearbyMask(st, i, j)];\n  if (!array) {\n    if (pound) return false;\n    writeFrames(st, i, j, 0, -1);\n    return false;\n  }\n  if (!pound) {\n    let num6 = -1, num7 = -1;\n    let flag = false;\n    for (let k = 0; k < array.length; k++) {\n      const num8 = array[k];\n      if (num2 === array[k]) num5 = k;\n      if (trackTypeOf(num8) !== num3) continue;\n      if (leftConnOf(num8) === NO_CONNECTION || rightConnOf(num8) === NO_CONNECTION) {\n        if (num === array[k]) { num4 = k; flag = true; }\n        if (num6 === -1) num6 = k;\n      } else {\n        if (num === array[k]) { num4 = k; flag = false; }\n        if (num7 === -1) num7 = k;\n      }\n    }\n    if (num7 !== -1) {\n      if (num4 === -1 || flag) num4 = num7;\n    } else {\n      if (num4 === -1) {\n        // 加速/压板轨无同型全连接项 → 保持现状（Minecart.cs:1032-1038 return false）\n        if (num3 === TYPE_BOOSTER) return false;\n        if (num3 === TYPE_PRESSURE) return false;\n        num4 = num6;\n      }\n      num5 = -1;\n    }\n  } else {\n    for (let l = 0; l < array.length; l++) {\n      if (num === array[l]) num4 = l;\n      if (num2 === array[l]) num5 = l;\n    }\n    let num9 = 0, num10 = 0;\n    for (let m = 0; m < array.length; m++) {\n      if (trackTypeOf(array[m]) !== num3) continue;\n      if (leftConnOf(array[m]) === NO_CONNECTION || rightConnOf(array[m]) === NO_CONNECTION) num10++;\n      else num9++;\n    }\n    if (num9 < 2 && num10 < 2) return false;\n    const flag2 = num9 === 0;\n    let flag3 = false;\n    if (!flag2) {\n      // 后轨循环：找下一个连接不同但同型且双侧全连接的项（Minecart.cs:1079-1094）\n      while (!flag3) {\n        num5++;\n        if (num5 >= array.length) { num5 = -1; break; }\n        if ((leftConnOf(array[num5]) !== leftConnOf(array[num4]) || rightConnOf(array[num5]) !== rightConnOf(array[num4]))\n          && trackTypeOf(array[num5]) === num3\n          && leftConnOf(array[num5]) !== NO_CONNECTION && rightConnOf(array[num5]) !== NO_CONNECTION) {\n          flag3 = true;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    if (!flag3) {\n      // 前轨循环（Minecart.cs:1096-1112；越界回卷后按同型同端型继续找，带安全边界）\n      do {\n        num4++;\n        if (num4 >= array.length) {\n          num4 = -1;\n          let guard = 0;\n          do {\n            num4++;\n            guard++;\n          } while (guard < array.length\n            && (trackTypeOf(array[num4]) !== num3\n              || ((leftConnOf(array[num4]) === NO_CONNECTION || rightConnOf(array[num4]) === NO_CONNECTION) !== flag2)));\n          break;\n        }\n      } while (trackTypeOf(array[num4]) !== num3\n        || ((leftConnOf(array[num4]) === NO_CONNECTION || rightConnOf(array[num4]) === NO_CONNECTION) !== flag2));\n    }\n  }\n  let flag4 = false;\n  switch (num4) {\n    case -2:\n      if (frontTrack(st, idx) !== FIRST_PRESSURE_FRAME) flag4 = true;\n      break;\n    case -1:\n      if (frontTrack(st, idx) !== 0) flag4 = true;\n      break;\n    default:\n      if (num4 >= 0 && num4 < array.length && frontTrack(st, idx) !== array[num4]) flag4 = true;\n      break;\n  }\n  if (num5 === -1) {\n    if (backTrack(st, idx) !== -1) flag4 = true;\n  } else if (num5 >= 0 && num5 < array.length && backTrack(st, idx) !== array[num5]) {\n    flag4 = true;\n  }\n  switch (num4) {\n    case -2: writeFrames(st, i, j, FIRST_PRESSURE_FRAME, num5 >= 0 && num5 < array.length ? array[num5] : -1); break;\n    case -1: writeFrames(st, i, j, 0, num5 >= 0 && num5 < array.length ? array[num5] : -1); break;\n    default:\n      if (num4 >= 0 && num4 < array.length) writeFrames(st, i, j, array[num4], num5 >= 0 && num5 < array.length ? array[num5] : -1);\n      break;\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** 放置后重 framing：自身 + 六邻（破坏/放置都要让邻接轨道重算连接） */\nexport function frameTrackArea(st: TileStore, i: number, j: number): void {\n  frameTrack(st, i, j, false);\n  for (const [dx, dy] of [[-1, -1], [-1, 0], [-1, 1], [1, -1], [1, 0], [1, 1]] as const) {\n    if (st.inBounds(i + dx, j + dy)) frameTrack(st, i + dx, j + dy, false);\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// FlipSwitchTrack（Minecart.cs:1302-1325）：电路信号到达压板/加速轨时翻向\n//   type 0：前/后轨互换（分叉换边）\n//   type 2：FrameTrack(pound) 循环下一个加速向\n// ============================================================================\nexport function flipSwitchTrack(st: TileStore, i: number, j: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return false;\n  const idx = st.idx(i, j);\n  if (!isTrackCell(st, idx)) return false;\n  const num = frontTrack(st, idx);\n  if (num === -1) return false;\n  switch (trackTypeOf(num)) {\n    case TYPE_NORMAL: {\n      const back = backTrack(st, idx);\n      if (back !== -1) {\n        writeFrames(st, i, j, back, num);\n        return true;\n      }\n      return false;\n    }\n    case TYPE_BOOSTER:\n      return frameTrack(st, i, j, true, true);\n    default:\n      return false;\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// PlaceTrack（Minecart.cs:1492-1512）：放置初始帧\n//   style 0 普通(-1) / 1 压板 / 2 左加速 / 3 右加速（随后 FrameTrack 定型）\n// ============================================================================\nexport function placeTrack(st: TileStore, i: number, j: number, style: number): void {\n  let front = -1;\n  switch (style) {\n    case 0: front = -1; break;\n    case 1: front = FIRST_PRESSURE_FRAME; break;\n    case 2: front = FIRST_LEFT_BOOST_FRAME; break;\n    case 3: front = FIRST_RIGHT_BOOST_FRAME; break;\n  }\n  st.setTile(i, j, TRACK_TILE, front < 0 ? 0xFFFF : front, 0xFFFF);\n}\n\n/** GetTrackItem（Minecart.cs:1514-1523）：帧 → 原版物品 id（2340/2492/2739） */\nexport function trackItemId(frame: number): number {\n  switch (trackTypeOf(frame)) {\n    case TYPE_PRESSURE: return 2492;\n    case TYPE_BOOSTER: return 2739;\n    default: return 2340;\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// 磁石位（Minecart.cs:1296-1300）：车身贴图参考点 = 位置 + 偏移 + MagnetOffset\n// ============================================================================\nexport function magnetPosition(x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number): { x: number; y: number } {\n  return {\n    x: x + width / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2 + CART_MAGNET_OFFSET.x,\n    y: y + height / 2 + CART_MAGNET_OFFSET.y,\n  };\n}\n\n/** OnTrack（Minecart.cs:1249-1259）：磁石位所在格是否轨道 */\nexport function onTrack(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number): boolean {\n  const m = magnetPosition(x, y, width, height);\n  const tx = Math.trunc(m.x / 16), ty = Math.trunc(m.y / 16);\n  if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return false;\n  const i = st.idx(tx, ty);\n  return st.type[i] === TRACK_TILE && !!(st.flags[i] & 1);\n}\n\n/** GetOnTrack（Minecart.cs:1204-1247）：右键/R 上轨——吸附到最近有效子段的轨面坐标 */\nexport function getOnTrack(\n  st: TileStore, tileX: number, tileY: number,\n  x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number,\n): { x: number; y: number } | null {\n  if (!st.inBounds(tileX, tileY)) return null;\n  const ti = st.idx(tileX, tileY);\n  if (st.type[ti] !== TRACK_TILE || !isTrackCell(st, ti)) return null;\n  const frame = frontTrack(st, ti);\n  const m = magnetPosition(x, y, width, height);\n  let num = Math.trunc(Math.trunc(m.x) % 16 / 2);\n  let num3 = 0, num2 = -1;\n  for (let i = num; i < 8; i++) {\n 轨道跟随运动学核心 1:1\n// 入参 rider 就地变更（Position/Velocity/lastBoost），返回 BitsByte 结果位。\n// ============================================================================\nexport interface TrackRider {\n  x: number; y: number;           // 位置（左上角，与玩家/实体同语义）\n  vx: number; vy: number;         // 速度\n  lastBoostX: number; lastBoostY: number;\n}\nexport interface TrackCollisionHooks {\n  /** false; followUp = false; }\n  const offX = width / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2;\n  const offY = height / 2;\n  const vector2 = { x: r.x + offX + CART_MAGNET_OFFSET.x, y: r.y + offY + CART_MAGNET_OFFSET.y };\n  const vector3 = { x: r.vx, y: r.vy };\n  let num = Math.hypot(vector3.x, vector3.y);\n  if (num > 0) { vector3.x /= num; vector3.y /= num; }\n  const vector4 = { x: // 实体与 Game 交互的接口（避免循环依赖，只放类型）
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface GameHooks {
  world: World;
  /** 主角(投射物命中判定用) */
  player: import('./Player').Player;
  /** 敌人列表(投射物命中判定用) */
  enemies(): unknown[];
  /** 小动物列表(弹幕命中判定用;近战挥砍在 Game 内直接遍历桶) */
  critters(): unknown[];
  /** 在世界上生成掉落物（返回实体便于染色等后处理） */
  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count?: number, vx?: number, vy?: number): import('./ItemDrop').ItemDrop | null;
  /** 对玩家造成伤害（attacker 用于荆棘反伤） */
  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: unknown): void;
  /** 伤害数字飘字 */
  addDamageNumber(x: number, y: number, amount: number, crit: boolean, color?: string): void;
  /** 弹幕砍 tileCut 物（Projectile.CutTiles 语义：箭扫过杂草/瓦罐 → Game 内部分流） */
  cutTile(x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 传送门弹 601 落门（PortalHelper.TryPlacingPortal + AddPortal；Game 实现入 props 桶） */
  placePortal?(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, form: number): void;
  /** 破坏 tile（掉落+音效+帧刷新；Enemy 攻门拆门 = WorldGen.KillTile 语义） */
  breakTile?(x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 敌人死亡 */
  onEnemyKilled(key: string, enemy?: import('../entities/Enemy').Enemy): void;
  /** 出生敌人 */
  spawnEnemy(key: string, x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 粒子 */
  spawnParticles(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed?: number,
    opts?: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number; size?: number },
  ): void;

 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/entities/Minecart.ts",
 "content": "// 矿车坐骑实体（原版语义 = Mount 6 \"Minecart\"：坐骑位锁定 + Minecart.cs 轨道跟随运动学）。\n// 原版矿车不是独立实体——玩家 mount 后玩家 hitbox 即车（20×52，heightBoost 10），\n// 每帧走 Player.cs:27783-27850 的 TrackCollision 段。本作以 props 实体承载同一状态，\n// fixedUpdate 跑 1:1 运动学，玩家位置每 tick 回写（Player.ridingCart 封常规移动）。\n//\n// 坐骑参数（Mount.cs mounts[6] / SuperCart 常量）：\n//   runSpeed 13 / dashSpeed 13 / acceleration 0.04 / jumpHeight 15 / jumpSpeed 5.15\n//   升级套件（5289 解锁 UsingSuperCart）：20 / 20 / 0.1 / 15 / 5.15\nimport { Entity } from './Entity';\nimport type { GameHooks } from './types';\nimport type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';\nimport type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';\nimport {\n  trackCollision, trackRotation, onTrack,\n  FLAG_ON_TRACK, FLAG_BOUNCY_BUMPER, FLAG_USED_RAMP, FLAG_HIT_SWITCH,\n  FLAG_BOOST_LEFT, FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT,\n  CART_RUN_SPEED, CART_DASH_SPEED, CART_ACCELERATION,\n  CART_JUMP_HEIGHT, CART_JUMP_SPEED,\n  SUPER_CART_RUN_SPEED, SUPER_CART_DASH_SPEED, SUPER_CART_ACCELERATION,\n  SUPER_CART_JUMP_HEIGHT, SUPER_CART_JUMP_SPEED,\n  TRACK_BOOST_DELTA, PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY, RUN_SLOWDOWN,\n  CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH,\n} from '../world/MinecartTrack';\n\n/** 矿车族物品（vanilla id）→ 坐骑贴图文件（Mount.cs mounts[n].frontTexture） */\nexport const CART_TEXTURES: Record<number, string> = {\n  2343: 'vanilla/Mount_Minecart.png',          // Minecart（mount 6）\n  3353: 'vanilla/Mount_MinecartMech.png',      // Mechanical Cart（mount 11）\n  4066: 'vanilla/Mount_MinecartDesert.png',    // Desert Minecart（mount 15）\n  4426: 'vanilla/Extra_108.png',               // Bee Minecart（mount 18）\n  4427: 'vanilla/Extra_109.png',               // Ladybug Minecart（mount 19）\n  4428: 'vanilla/Extra_110.png',               // Pigron Minecart（mount 20）\n  4429: 'vanilla/Extra_111.png',               // Sunflower Minecart（mount 21）\n  4443: 'vanilla/Extra_112.png',               // Demonic Hellcart（mount 22）\n  4450: 'vanilla/Extra_115.png',               // Shroom Minecart（mount 24）\n};\nexport const DEFAULT_CART_TEXTURE = 'vanilla/Mount_Minecart.png';\n\nexport class Minecart extends Entity {\n  // 原版坐骑 hitbox：width 20、height 42 + heightBoost 10 = 52（Mount.cs mounts[6]）\n  w = 20;\n  h = 52;\n  mounted = false;          // 玩家骑乘中\n  dead = false;\n  /** 坐骑贴图（Mount.frontTexture 文件名，atlas.ensureVImage 懒加载） */\n  texture = DEFAULT_CART_TEXTURE;\n  /** 升级套件已解锁（Player.UsingSuperCart → SuperCart* 常量） */\n  superCart = false;\n  /** 车身朝向翻转（撞弹性挡板时随输入翻；Mount._type 6 绘制翻转语义近似） */\n  cartFlip = false;\n  /** 车身倾角（Player.fullRotation = TrackRotation 的 atan2） */\n  rotation = 0;\n  /** 纵向贴轨绘制偏移（TrackRotation 返回值 = Player.gfxOffY） */\n  gfxOffY = 0;\n  /** 本 tick 是否在轨（Player.onTrack） */\n  onTrackNow = false;\n  /** 跳跃剩余 tick（Player.jump = Mount.JumpHeight；每 tick vy=-jumpSpeed） */\n  jump = 0;\n  jumpSpeed = CART_JUMP_SPEED;\n  /** 加速轨道累积（Player.trackBoost：下帧并入 vx，钳 ±maxRunSpeed） */\n  trackBoost = 0;\n  /** 斜坡起飞动画计时（Player.cartRampTime = min(1,|vx|/RunSpeed)*20） */\n  cartRampTime = 0;\n  /** 轮组动画 tick（Mount runningFrame 3 帧 × delay 12） */\n  animTick = 0;\n  /** TrackCollision 的 lastBoost（加速轨去重：同格不重复给速） */\n  lastBoostX = 0;\n  lastBoostY = 0;\n  /** 落轨音效的 fallStart（Player.fallStart：开始下落的 tile Y） */\n  fallStart = 0;\n\n  private wasOnTrack = false;\n\n  /** 坐骑参数（SuperCart 覆盖） */\n  get runSpeed(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_RUN_SPEED : CART_RUN_SPEED; }\n  get dashSpeed(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_DASH_SPEED : CART_DASH_SPEED; }\n  get acceleration(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_ACCELERATION : CART_ACCELERATION; }\n  get jumpHeight(): number { return this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_JUMP_HEIGHT : CART_JUMP_HEIGHT; }\n\n  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {\n    // 骑乘中由 Renderer.drawPlayer 叠画（原版 frontTexture 在玩家之前层）；\n    // 未骑乘的矿车原版不存在（坐骑 buff 结束即消失）——保留贴图绘制仅作调试残留态\n    if (this.mounted) return;\n    const img = r.assets?.atlas?.ensureVImage(this.texture);\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx || !img) return;\n    const fh = Math.floor(img.height / 3);\n    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, img.width, fh,\n      this.x + this.w / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - fh / 2,\n      img.width, fh);\n  }\n\n  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    if (!this.mounted) { this.dead = true; return; }   // 坐骑语义：无骑乘即消亡\n    const p = game.player;\n    const st = game.world.store;\n    const maxRun = this.runSpeed;\n\n    // ---- trackBoost 并入（Player.cs:19530-19544：钳 ±maxRunSpeed）----\n    if (this.trackBoost !== 0) {\n      this.vx += this.trackBoost;\n      this.trackBoost = 0;\n      if (this.vx < -maxRun) this.vx = -maxRun;\n      else if (this.vx > maxRun) this.vx = maxRun;\n    }\n\n    // ---- 水平输入（Player.cs:19546-19690 Carts 段 1:1）----\n    // 车只在贴轨（vy==0）时响应加速/反推；runSlowdown+runAcceleration 同帧叠加\n    const controlLeft = p.inputX < 0, controlRight = p.inputX > 0;\n    if (controlLeft && this.vx > -maxRun) {\n      if (this.vy === 0) {\n        if (this.vx > RUN_SLOWDOWN) this.vx -= RUN_SLOWDOWN;\n        this.vx -= this.acceleration;\n      }\n    } else if (controlRight && this.vx < maxRun) {\n      if (this.vy === 0) {\n        if (this.vx < -RUN_SLOWDOWN) this.vx += RUN_SLOWDOWN;\n        this.vx += this.acceleration;\n      }\n    }\n    // 朝向：仅输入方向与速度同号才转（Player.cs:19553-19561 mount.Cart 门）\n    if (p.inputX !== 0 && Math.sign(this.vx) === p.inputX) p.facing = p.inputX;\n\n    // ---- 跳跃（Player.JumpMovement :20402-20418 + Mount.JumpSpeed/JumpHeight）----\n    this.jumpSpeed = (this.superCart ? SUPER_CART_JUMP_SPEED : CART_JUMP_SPEED);\n    if (p.inputJump) {\n      if (this.jump > 0) {\n        if (this.vy === 0) this.jump = 0;\n        else { this.vy = -this.jumpSpeed; this.jump--; }\n      } else if (this.vy === 0 && this.onTrackNow) {\n        // MountID.Sets.Cart 起跳：位置微抬防立即重贴轨（:20406 position.Y -= 0.001）\n        this.y -= 0.001;\n        this.jump = this.jumpHeight;\n        this.vy = -this.jumpSpeed;\n      }\n    } else {\n      this.jump = 0;\n    }\n\n    // ---- 重力（Player.gravity = defaultGravity 0.4；在轨时 TrackCollision 清零 vy）----\n    if (this.vy === 0) this.fallStart = Math.trunc(this.y / 16);\n    this.vy += PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY;\n\n    // ---- TrackCollision（Player.cs:27795-27850 1:1）----\n    this.wasOnTrack = this.onTrackNow;\n    const prevVy = this.vy;\n    const rider = {\n      x: this.x, y: this.y, vx: this.vx, vy: this.vy,\n      lastBoostX: this.lastBoostX, lastBoostY: this.lastBoostY,\n    };\n    const bits = trackCollision(\n      st, rider, this.w, this.h,\n      !!p.inputDown, !!p.inputUp, this.fallStart, false,\n      {\n        landing: (lx, ly) => {\n          // MinecartLandingSound（DelegateMethods.Minecart.LandingSound → SoundID.Item3）\n          game.playSfx('minecart_land', 0.7, lx, ly);\n          game.spawnParticles(lx, ly + this.h - 4, '#C8B08A', 6, 1.2, { life: 24 });\n        },\n      },\n    );\n    this.x = rider.x; this.y = rider.y; this.vx = rider.vx; this.vy = rider.vy;\n    this.lastBoostX = rider.lastBoostX; this.lastBoostY = rider.lastBoostY;\n\n    this.onTrackNow = !!(bits & (1 << FLAG_ON_TRACK));\n    if (this.onTrackNow) {\n      // 倾角 + gfxOffY（Player.cs:27804-27806：位置前探一步取两轮轨面）\n      const rot = { v: this.rotation };\n      this.gfxOffY = trackRotation(st, this.x + this.vx, this.y + this.vy, this.w, this.h,\n        !!p.inputDown, !!p.inputUp, rot);\n      this.rotation = rot.v;\n      if (this.vy === 0) this.cartRampTime = 0;   // Player.cs:26499-26501\n      if (prevVy > PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY && this.vy === 0) this.fallStart = Math.trunc(this.y / 16);\n    } else {\n      this.rotation = 0;\n      this.gfxOffY = 0;\n      if (this.wasOnTrack) {\n        // 跳车音效（DelegateMethods.Minecart.JumpingSound → SoundID.Item3）\n        game.playSfx('minecart_jump', 0.6, this.x, this.y);\n      }\n    }\n    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOUNCY_BUMPER)) {\n      if (controlLeft || controlRight) this.cartFlip = !this.cartFlip;\n      if (this.vx > 0) p.facing = 1;\n      else if (this.vx < 0) p.facing = -1;\n      game.playSfx('minecart_bumper', 0.8, this.x, this.y);\n    }\n    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_USED_RAMP)) {\n      this.cartRampTime = Math.trunc(Math.min(1, Math.abs(this.vx) / this.dashSpeed) * 20);\n    }\n    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_HIT_SWITCH)) {\n      // Player.cs:27927-27931 → Minecart.HitTrackSwitch → Wiring.HitSwitch(磁石位)\n      const mx = Math.trunc((this.x + this.w / 2 - CART_TEXTURE_WIDTH / 2 + 25) / 16);\n      const my = Math.trunc((this.y + this.h / 2 + 26) / 16);\n      game.hitTrackSwitch?.(mx, my);\n    }\n    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_LEFT)) this.trackBoost -= TRACK_BOOST_DELTA;\n    if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT)) this.trackBoost += TRACK_BOOST_DELTA;\n\n    if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.1) this.animTick++;\n    else this.animTick = 0;\n\n    // ---- 玩家坐骑位回写（原版玩家 hitbox 即车；此处锁玩家到车位）----\n    p.x = this.x;\n    p.y = this.y;\n    p.vx = this.vx;\n    p.vy = 0;\n    p.onGround = this.onTrackNow;\n    p.fallStartY = null;   // 车上不计摔伤（原版 mount fallStart 语义）\n\n    // 完全脱轨且离轨远处（误用兜底）：无轨重力自然下落由 TileCollision 缺失 → 消亡防穿地\n    if (!this.onTrackNow && this.vy > 0 && !onTrack(st, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h)\n      && this.y > game.world.store.h * 16 - 64) {\n      this.dead = true;\n      this.mounted = false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 轮组动画帧（Mount runningFrameCount 3 / runningFrameDelay 12；静止 standingFrame 0） */\n  get animFrame(): number {\n    return Math.abs(this.vx) > 0.1 ? Math.floor(this.animTick / 12) % 3 : 0;\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T07:49:00.012Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Issues:
1. `r.assets?.atlas?.ensureVImage` — need to check Renderer API (r.assets, this.atlas). Renderer has `this.atlas`. I'll fix draw to use `r.atlas?.ensureVImage(...)`. Check Renderer fields: `this.atlas` used at 3153 (`this.atlas`). Also `r.canvas` used before. Fix.

2. fallStart semantics: vanilla fallStart is set when player starts falling (`fallStart = (int)(position.Y/16)` when velocity.Y becomes > gravity?). I approximated. Fine.

3. On jump: vanilla requires releaseJump (edge) for the initial jump? Looking at JumpMovement: `(sliding || velocity.Y == 0f || ...) && (releaseJump || (autoJump && ...))` — yes! Initial jump requires releaseJump (fresh press), but `jump > 0` continuation works while held. My code: `else if (this.vy === 0 && this.onTrackNow)` triggers while held → auto-bounce repeatedly (autoJump behavior). Vanilla default autoJump=false → requires release. Need jump press edge. The game's player uses `inputJump` continuous for ground jumps too (no edge requirement — holding space bunny hops). For the cart, to be 1:1 I need a release latch: add `jumpRelease` tracking: jump starts only if !prevJumpHeld. I'll track `private prevJump = false`.

Let me fix these in the entity.
```

</details>

```ts
export function drawMinecartTrackCell(ctx, atlas, st, x, y, px, py, fxRaw, fyRaw) {
  const back = fyRaw === 0xFFFF ? -1 : fyRaw;
  const anim = boosterAnimFrame();
  const draw = (frameID: number, ox: number, oy: number) => {
    const s = sourceRectOf(frameID, anim);
    if (!s) return;
    const r = atlas.vrect(314, s.sx, s.sy, 16, 16);
    if (r) ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, 16, 16, px+ox, py+oy, 16, 16);
  };
  if (back >= 0) draw(back, 0, 0);
  draw(fxRaw >= 40 ? 0 : fxRaw, 0, 0);
  if (back >= 0 && drawLeftDecoration(back)) draw(DECOR_LEFT_DOWN, 0, 16);
  if (drawLeftDecoration(fxRaw)) draw(DECOR_LEFT_DOWN, 0, 16);
  if (back >= 0 && drawRightDecoration(back)) draw(DECOR_RIGHT_DOWN, 0, 16);
  if (drawRightDecoration(fxRaw)) draw(DECOR_RIGHT_DOWN, 0, 16);
  if (drawBumper(fxRaw)) draw(DECOR_REGULAR_BUMPER, 0, -16);
  else if (drawBouncyBumper(fxRaw)) draw(DECOR_BOUNCY_BUMPER, 0, -16);
}
```

'';\n    if (key === 'minecart_item') return DEFAULT_CART_TEXTURE;\n    const vid = viIdFromKey(key);\n    return vid >= 0 ? CART_TEXTURES[vid] ?? null : null;\n  }\n\n  /** 上车（TrackHook proj 403 语义：GetOnTrack 吸附 + 落点实心门 :22407）。\n   *  返回 false = 落点被实心卡住，上车失败 */\n  private mountMinecartAt(tx: number, ty: number, texture: string): boolean {\n    if (this.minecart) return false;\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const cart = new Minecart();\n    cart.texture = texture;\n    cart.superCart = this.superCartUnlocked();\n    const snap = getOnTrack(st, tx, ty, this.player.x, this.player.y, cart.w, cart.h);\n    if (snap) { cart.x = snap.x; cart.y = snap.y; }\n    else { cart.x = tx * TILE + 8 - cart.w / 2; cart.y = (ty + 1) * TILE - cart.h; }\n    // 落点实心门（Projectile 403：!Collision.SolidCollision(Position, width, height-20)）\n    for (let sx = Math.floor(cart.x / TILE); sx <= Math.floor((cart.x + cart.w - 1) / TILE); sx++) {\n      for (let sy = Math.floor((cart.y + 20) / TILE); sy <= Math.floor((cart.y + cart.h - 1) / TILE); sy++) {\n        if (st.inBounds(sx, sy) && st.isSolid(sx, sy)) return false;\n      }\n    }\n    cart.mounted = true;\n    this.entities.add(cart, 'props');\n    this.minecart = cart;\n    this.player.ridingCart = true;\n    this.player.cart = cart;\n    this.sfx.play('minecart', 0.7);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** 下车（Mount.TryDismount：位置保持、坐骑消失——矿车 buff 结束即移除） */\n  private dismountMinecart(): void {\n    const cart = this.minecart;\n    if (!cart) return;\n    const p = this.player;\n    p.x = cart.x;\n    p.y = cart.y;\n    p.vx = 0;\n    p.vy = 0;\n    p.ridingCart = false;\n    p.cart = null;\n    cart.mounted = false;\n    cart.dead = true;\n    this.minecart = null;\n  }\n\n  /** QuickMinecart（Player.cs:5796-5827）：射程内取视线可达的最近轨道格上车 */\n  private tryQuickMinecart(): void {\n    if (this.minecart) return;\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const p = this.player;\n    const ptx = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(p.cy / TILE);\n    const reach = this.inTileRangeReach();\n    let best: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;\n    let bestD = Infinity;\n    for (let x = ptx - reach; x <= ptx + reach; x++) {\n      for (let y = pty - reach; y <= pty + reach; y++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== this.trackTile) continue;\n        const d = Math.hypot(x * TILE + 8 - p.cx, y * TILE + 8 - p.cy);\n        if (d < bestD && lineOfSight(st, p.cx, p.cy, x * TILE + 8, y * TILE + 8)) {\n          bestD = d;\n          best = { x, y };\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    if (best) {\n      // 贴图优先级：矿车装备槽（miscEquips[2]）> 手持 > 背包首件（TrackHook 近似）\n      const tex = this.bestCartTexture();\n      this.mountMinecartAt(best.x, best.y, tex);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 玩家可用的矿车贴图（miscEquips[2] 装备矿车 > 手持 > 背包扫描；无则基础矿车） */\n  private bestCartTexture(): string {\n    const misc = this.player.inv.miscEquips?.[2];\n    if (misc) {\n      const t = this.cartTextureOfItem(misc.id);\n      if (t) return t;\n    }\n    const held = this.player.inv.heldItem();\n    if (held) {\n      const t = this.cartTextureOfItem(held.id);\n      if (t) return t;\n    }\n    for (const s of this.player.inv.slots) {\n      if (!s) continue;\n      const t = this.cartTextureOfItem(s.id);\n      if (t) return t;\n    }\n    return DEFAULT_CART_TEXTURE;\n  }\n\n  /** 矿车升级套件解锁态（Player.unlockedSuperCart；持久化于世界 flags） */\n  private superCartUnlocked(): boolean {\n    return !!(this.world.flags as Record<string, boolean>).superCart;\n  }"
}

115:  preloadNames(names: SfxName[]) {
export type SfxName =
  | 'hit' | 'hurt' | 'pickup' | 'dig' | 'place' | 'drink' | 'chop' | 'splash'
  | 'killed' | 'pkilled' | 'zombie' | 'roar' | 'coin' | 'door_open' | 'door_close' | 'tink' | 'shatter' | 'drown'
  | 'menuOpen' | 'menuClose' | 'menuTick'
  | 'bowShoot'   // 原版 SoundID.Item5（弓弦，Item.cs case 99 UseSound）
  | 'throw'      // 原版 SoundID.Item1（手里剑/投掷刀 UseSound）
  | 'explosion'  // 原版 SoundID.Item14（爆炸通用：手雷/炸弹/炸药/巨石,Projectile.cs 20+ 处）
  | 'beeSummon'  // 原版 SoundID.Item173（Abeemination 蜂后召唤音，Player.cs:43185——Boss 召唤音唯一非 Roar 例外）
  | 'thunder'   // 原版 SoundID 43（Thunder_0-5，NewLightning 的 thunderDelay 归零时播）
--- canHitLine sig
export function canHitLine(st: Store, x1: number, y1: number, w1: number, h1: number,
  x2: number, y2: number, w2: number, h2: number): boolean {
  const [num, num2, num3, num4] = centerTiles(st, x1, y1, w1, h1, x2, y2, w2, h2);
  const d5 = Math.abs(num - num3), d6 = Math.abs(num2 - num4);
  if (d5 === 0 && d6 === 0) return true;
  let stepX = 1, stepY = 1;                                   // num7/num8
  if (d5 === 0) stepX = 0;
  else if (d6 === 0) stepY = 0;

 "new_string": "  /** QuickMinecart（Player.cs:5796-5827）：射程内取视线可达的最近轨道格上车 */\n  private tryQuickMinecart(): void {\n    if (this.minecart) return;\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const p = this.player;\n    const ptx = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(p.cy / TILE);\n    // TileReachCheckSettings.Simple 同款射程盒（inTileRange 口径：x 5 / y 3 + tileBoost）\n    const tb = (p.equipStats.tileRange ? 3 : 0) + (p.buffs.has(BuffType.Builder) ? 1 : 0);\n    const rx = Math.min(20, 5) + tb, ry = Math.min(20, 3) + tb;\n    let best: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;\n    let bestD = Infinity;\n    for (let x = ptx - rx; x <= ptx + rx; x++) {\n      for (let y = pty - ry; y <= pty + ry; y++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== this.trackTile) continue;\n        const wx = x * TILE + 8, wy = y * TILE + 8;\n        const d = Math.hypot(wx - p.cx, wy - p.cy);\n        if (d < bestD && canHitLine(st, p.cx, p.cy, 0, 0, wx, wy, 0, 0)) {\n          bestD = d;\n          best = { x, y };\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    if (best) {\n      // 贴图优先级：矿车装备槽（miscEquips[2]）> 手持 > 背包首件（TrackHook 近似）\n      const tex = this.bestCartTexture();\n      this.mountMinecartAt(best.x, best.y, tex);\n    }\n  }"
}

 "new_string": "/** 矿车轨道格绘制（TileDrawing.DrawTile_MinecartTrack :3823-3857 1:1）。\n *  frameX = 前轨连接 ID、frameY = 后轨连接 ID（0xFFFF = -1 无后轨）。\n *  层序：后轨 → 前轨 → 下行装饰(36/37, 画在下一格) → 挡板(39 普通 / 38 弹性, 画在上一格)。\n *  加速轨道（type 2）随 boosterAnimFrame() 滚动帧行（Main.cs:18734-18741） */\nfunction drawMinecartTrackCell(\n  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas,\n  x: number, y: number, px: number, py: number, fx: number, fy: number,\n): void {\n  const back = fy === 0xFFFF ? -1 : fy;\n  const front = fx >= 40 ? 0 : fx;\n  const anim = boosterAnimFrame();\n  const draw = (frameID: number, ox: number, oy: number): void => {\n    const s = sourceRectOf(frameID, anim);\n    if (!s) return;\n    const r = atlas.vrect(314, s.sx, s.sy, 16, 16);\n    if (r) ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, 16, 16, px + ox, py + oy, TILE, TILE);\n  };\n  if (back >= 0) draw(back, 0, 0);\n  draw(front, 0, 0);\n  if (back >= 0 && drawLeftDecoration(back)) draw(DECOR_LEFT_DOWN, 0, TILE);\n  if (drawLeftDecoration(front)) draw(DECOR_LEFT_DOWN, 0, TILE);\n  if (back >= 0 && drawRightDecoration(back)) draw(DECOR_RIGHT_DOWN, 0, TILE);\n  if (drawRightDecoration(front)) draw(DECOR_RIGHT_DOWN, 0, TILE);\n  if (drawBumper(front)) draw(DECOR_REGULAR_BUMPER, 0, -TILE);\n  else if (drawBouncyBumper(front)) draw(DECOR_BOUNCY_BUMPER, 0, -TILE);\n  void x; void y;\n}\n\n/** 绘制一格原版图块。"
}

export function swayBakeSkip(type: number, frameX: number): boolean {
  const r = SWAY_REC.get(type);
  if (!r) {
    // 滴水锚点（373/374/375/461/709）：原版主循环 continue 永不绘制——烘焙同样跳过
    const v = TILE_DEFS[type]?.vanilla;
    return !!v && NO_DRAW_SHEETS.has(v.sheet);
  }
  if (r.sheet === 530 && frameX >= 270) return false;
  if (r.sheet === 705 && frameX % 486 >= 270) return false;
  if (r.sheet === 227 && SWAY_227_EXCLUDE_FX.has(frameX)) return false;

```

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import { torchAnchorFrame, torchStillAnchored } from '../src/world/Torch';
import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

// 火把锚定（TileObjectData tile4 + TileObject.CanPlace 1:1）
function mkStore(w = 8, h = 8): TileStore {
  const st = new TileStore(w, h);
  return st;
}

describe('火把锚定（torchAnchorFrame）', () => {
  it('底锚优先：下方实心 → frameX 0（TileObject base 先于 alternates）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setTile(4, 4, T.STONE);
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('左锚：无地面但左邻实心 → frameX 22（AnchorLeft）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setTile(3, 3, T.STONE);
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(22);
  });

  it('右锚：仅右邻实心 → frameX 44（AnchorRight）', () => {
    const st = mkStore();
    st.setTile(5, 3, T.STONE);
    expect(torchAnchorFrame(st, 4, 3)).toBe(44);
  });

```

k < n; k++) {\n    placeTrack(st, x0 + k, y, style);\n    frameTrackArea(st, x0 + k, y);\n  }\n}\nfunction rider(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number): TrackRider {\n  return { x, y, vx, vy, lastBoostX: 0, lastBoostY: 0 };\n}\n/** 坐骑 hitbox（Mount：20×52）落在平轨轨面上的初始位：轨面 = y*16+14（frame 1 高度 14），\n *  磁石位 = 位 + (10,52) → 位.y = y*16+14-52 */\nfunction onFlat(x: number, ty: number, vx: number): TrackRider {\n  return rider(x, ty * 16 + 14 - 52, vx, PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY);\n}\n\ndescribe('帧族数据表（Minecart.Initialize 1:1）', () => {\n  it('平轨 frame 1：左右皆 MIDDLE、无贴轨偏移', () => {\n    expect(leftConnOf(1)).toBe(1);\n    expect(rightConnOf(1)).toBe(1);\n    expect(trackTypeOf(1)).toBe(0);\n    for (let s = 0; s < 8; s++) expect(tileHeightAt(1, s)).toBe(14);\n  });\n  it('斜坡 frame 8：左 TOP 右 BOTTOM、高度 2px 步进 0→14', () => {\n    expect(leftConnOf(8)).toBe(0);\n    expect(rightConnOf(8)).toBe(2);\n    expect(tileHeightAt(8, 0)).toBe(0);\n    expect(tileHeightAt(8, 7)).toBe(14);\n  });\n  it('压板 20-23 / 加速 30-35 分型正确', () => {\n    for (const f of [20, 21, 22, 23]) expect(trackTypeOf(f)).toBe(1);\n    for (const f of [30, 31, 32, 33, 34, 35]) expect(trackTypeOf(f)).toBe(2);\n    expect(isPressureFrame(20)).toBe(true);\n    expect(isPressureFrame(21)).toBe(true);\n    expect(isPressureFrame(1)).toBe(false);\n  });\n  it('GetTrackItem：2340 普通 / 2492 压板 / 2739 加速', () => {\n    expect(trackItemId(1)).toBe(2340);\n    expect(trackItemId(21)).toBe(2492);\n    expect(trackItemId(30)).toBe(2739);\n  });\n  it('GetSourceRect：纹理位 = 帧 ID × 18px 网格；加速帧行随动画滚动', () => {\n    const f1 = sourceRectOf(1)!;\n    expect(f1.sx).toBe(18);\n    expect(f1.sy).toBe(0);\n    const boost = sourceRectOf(30, 3)!;\n    expect(boost.sx).toBe(36);\n    expect(boost.sy).toBe(54 + 18 * 3);\n    // 装饰帧 ID ≥ 36 忽略动画参数（Minecart.cs:1533 的 frameID<36 门）\n    const decor = sourceRectOf(37, 4)!;\n    expect(decor.sy).toBe(108);\n    expect(boosterAnimFrame()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n    expect(boosterAnimFrame()).toBeLessThan(5);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('FrameTrack 放置定型（Minecart.FrameTrack 1:1）', () => {\n  it('孤格 → 开放端 frame 0（左右皆 -1）', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    placeTrack(st, 8, 8, 0);\n    frameTrackArea(st, 8, 8);\n    expect(st.frameX[st.idx(8, 8)]).toBe(0);\n    expect(st.frameY[st.idx(8, 8)]).toBe(0xFFFF);\n  });\n  it('三连直轨：中段 frame 1（左右平接）、两端开放端盖 14/15', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    line(st, 8, 8, 3);\n    const f = (x: number): number => st.frameX[st.idx(x, 8)];\n    expect(f(8)).toBe(14);   // Minecart.cs:911\n    expect(r.vx).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  });\n  it('弹性挡板（-2）：水平反弹 + FLAG_BouncyBumper', () => {\n    const st = mkStore(40, 24);\n    // 平轨 + 右端弹性挡板帧 25（L=1,R=-1，右端 -2）\n    line(st, 8, 12, 2);\n    st.setTile(10, 12, TRACK, 25, 0xFFFF);\n    const r = onFlat(8 * 16, 12, 7);\n    let bounced = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 40 && !bounced; t++) {\n      r.vy = PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY;\n      const bits = trackCollision(st, r, 20, 52, false, false, 0, false);\n      if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOUNCY_BUMPER)) bounced = true;\n    }\n    expect(bounced).toBe(true);\n    expect(r.vx).toBe(-7);    // Minecart.cs:920 Velocity.X 取反\n  });\n  it('加速轨道：BOOST 位 + lastBoost 同格去重', () => {\n    const st = mkStore(40, 24);\n    // 左侧平轨 + 加速带（frame 30 = boostRight）+ 右侧平轨\n    line(st, 8, 12, 2);\n    st.setTile(10, 12, TRACK, 30, 0xFFFF);\n    st.setTile(11, 12, TRACK, 1, 0xFFFF);\n    frameTrackArea(st, 10, 12);\n    frameTrackArea(st, 11, 12);\n    // frameTrack 可能把 30 定型为其他同型帧——只要还是加速型即可\n    const boostFrame = st.frameX[st.idx(10, 12)];\n    expect(trackTypeOf(boostFrame)).toBe(2);\n    const r = onFlat(8 * 16, 12, 3);\n    let boosted = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60 && !boosted; t++) {\n      r.vy = PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY;\n      const bits = trackCollision(st, r, 20, 52, false, false, 0, false);\n      if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_LEFT) || bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT)) {\n        boosted = true;\n        // 同 tick 内不会再给（lastBoost 已记）——下一 tick 仍在原格时不再置位\n        r.vy = 0;\n        r.vx = 0;\n        r.x -= 0.001;   // 原地不动（vx=0 → 不跨格）\n        const again = trackCollision(st, r, 20, 52, false, false, 0, false);\n        expect(again & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_LEFT)).toBeFalsy();\n        expect(again & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT)).toBeFalsy();\n      }\n    }\n    expect(boosted).toBe(true);\n  });\n  it('压板轨道：FLAG_HitSwitch（Wiring.HitSwitch 钩）', () => {\n    const st = mkStore(40, 24);\n    line(st, 8, 12, 2);\n    st.setTile(10, 12, TRACK, 21, 0xFFFF);   // 压板平段\n    const r = onFlat(8 * 16, 12, 4);\n    let hit = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60 && !hit; t++) {\n      r.vy = PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY;\n      const bits = trackCollision(st, r, 20, 52, false, false, 0, false);\n      if (bits & (1 << 3)) hit = true;   // FLAG_HIT_SWITCH\n    }\n    expect(hit).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('上轨吸附（Minecart.GetOnTrack / OnTrack 1:1）', () => {\n  it('GetOnTrack：吸附到最近有效子段的轨面', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    flat(st, 8, 8);\n    const pos = getOnTrack(st, 8, 8, 8 * 16 + 4, 8 * 16 - 20, 20, 52);\n    expect(pos).not.toBeNull();\n    // 轨面 y = 8*16+14；pos.y + 52/2 + 26 = 轨面\n    expect(pos!.y + 52 / 2 + 26).toBeCloseTo(8 * 16 + 14, 1);\n  });\n  it('OnTrack：磁石位所在格判定', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    flat(st, 8, 8);\n    // 磁石位 = (x+10, y+52) 落在 (8,8)\n    expect(onTrack(st, 8 * 16 + 4 - 10 + 2, 8 * 16 + 14 - 52, 20, 52)).toBe(true);\n    expect(onTrack(st, 2 * 16, 2 * 16, 20, 52)).toBe(false);\n  });\n  it('端点开放帧（frame 0 无有效子段）不可吸附', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    st.setTile(8, 8, TRACK, 0, 0xFFFF);\n    const pos = getOnTrack(st, 8, 8, 8 * 16, 8 * 16, 20, 52);\n    expect(pos).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n"

TRACK_TYPE[num] : TYPE_NORMAL;"
}

 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tests/minecart-track.test.ts",
 "old_string": "/** 把骑手放到 (tx,ty) 格内指定子段高度的轨面上。\n *  磁石位 = (x + 10, y + 52)；x 取格左 +2px → 子段 1 */\nfunction seat(tx: number, ty: number, height: number, vx: number): TrackRider {\n  return rider(tx * 16 + 2, ty * 16 + height - 52, vx, PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY);\n}\n/** 平轨座位 */\nfunction seatFlat(tx: number, ty: number, vx: number): TrackRider {\n  return seat(tx, ty, FLAT_H, vx);\n}",
 "new_string": "/** 把骑手放到 (tx,ty) 格内指定子段的轨面上。\n *  磁石位 = (x + 10, y + 52)；x 使磁石落在子段 s 起点 → 高度取该帧该子段 */\nfunction seatSub(tx: number, ty: number, frame: number, s: number, vx: number): TrackRider {\n  return rider(tx * 16 + s * 2 - 10, ty * 16 + tileHeightAt(frame, s) - 52, vx, PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY);\n}\n/** 平轨座位（子段 1） */\nfunction seatFlat(tx: number, ty: number, vx: number): TrackRider {\n  return seatSub(tx, ty, 1, 1, vx);\n}\n/** 一步物理 = TrackCollision（改速度+贴轨 Y）+ 外部位置积分（原版 DryCollision 段） */\nfunction step(st: TileStore, r: TrackRider): number {\n  r.vy = PLAYER_DEFAULT_GRAVITY;   // 每帧先加重力（Player.Update 时序）\n  const bits = trackCollision(st, r, W, H, false, false, 0, false);\n  r.x += r.vx;\n  r.y += r.vy;\n  return bits;\n}"
}

k < 6; k++) seg(st, 10 + k, 10, 8);   // 左高右低 0→14\n    const r = seatSub(10, 10, 8, 1, 4);\n    const y0 = r.y;\n    let onTrackSeen = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60; t++) {\n      const bits = step(st, r);\n      if (bits & (1 << FLAG_ON_TRACK)) onTrackSeen = true;\n      if (r.x > 14 * 16) break;\n    }\n    expect(onTrackSeen).toBe(true);\n    expect(r.y).toBeGreaterThan(y0);   // 下行\n    expect(r.vx).toBeCloseTo(4, 3);    // 原版沿轨不改变速率\n  });\n  it('斜坡起飞端（RAMP_END -3）：脱轨跳跃 vy = -|vx|/√2（上行）、vx 保持', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    seg(st, 10, 10, 16);   // L=BOTTOM、子段 2-7 RAMP_END → 向右走上抛\n    const r = seatSub(10, 10, 16, 1, 8);   // 子段 1（高度 12）\n    let ramp = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 30 && !ramp; t++) {\n      const bits = step(st, r);\n      if (bits & (1 << FLAG_USED_RAMP)) ramp = true;\n    }\n    expect(ramp).toBe(true);\n    expect(r.vx).toBe(8);                                  // 水平保持（Minecart.cs:698）\n    expect(r.vy).toBeCloseTo(-8 * Math.SQRT1_2, 3);        // 垂直 = ±|vx|/√2\n  });\n  it('开放端（OPEN_END -4）：脱轨下落 vy = defaultGravity', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    line(st, 8, 12, 2);\n    seg(st, 10, 12, 14);   // 右端弹性挡板\n    const r = seatFlat(8, 12, 7);\n    let bounced = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 120 && !bounced; t++) {\n      const bits = step(st, r);\n      if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOUNCY_BUMPER)) bounced = true;\n    }\n    expect(bounced).toBe(true);\n    expect(r.vx).toBe(-7);   // Minecart.cs:920 Velocity.X 取反\n  });\n  it('加速轨道：BOOST 位 + lastBoost 同格去重', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    line(st, 8, 12, 2);\n    seg(st, 10, 12, 30);    // boostRight（frame 30）\n    seg(st, 11, 12, 1);\n    frameTrackArea(st, 10, 12);\n    const boostFrame = st.frameX[st.idx(10, 12)];\n    expect(trackTypeOf(boostFrame)).toBe(2);\n    const r = seatFlat(8, 12, 3);\n    let boosted = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 120 && !boosted; t++) {\n      const bits = step(st, r);\n      if (bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_LEFT) || bits & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT)) {\n        boosted = true;\n        // 原地不动（vx=0 → 不跨格）：lastBoost 已记同格 → 不再置位\n        const save = { x: r.x, y: r.y };\n        r.vx = 0; r.vy = 0;\n        const again = trackCollision(st, r, W, H, false, false, 0, false);\n        expect(again & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_LEFT)).toBeFalsy();\n        expect(again & (1 << FLAG_BOOST_RIGHT)).toBeFalsy();\n        r.x = save.x; r.y = save.y;\n      }\n    }\n    expect(boosted).toBe(true);\n  });\n  it('压板轨道：FLAG_HitSwitch（Wiring.HitSwitch 钩）', () => {\n    const st = mkStore();\n    line(st, 8, 12, 2);\n    seg(st, 10, 12, 21); #!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
    const path = await import('node:path');
    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------
(eval):1: no matches found: tools/_diag-*
./_diag-minecart-debug.mts

```

    // maxRunSpeed 上限\n    expect(cart.x).toBeGreaterThan(x0 + 100);\n    expect(p.x).toBe(cart.x);                    // 坐骑位锁定\n    expect(p.y).toBe(cart.y);\n    expect(p.onGround).toBe(true);\n    expect(p.vy).toBe(0);\n  });\n  it('松开输入：速度保持（原版车上无摩擦，Player.cs:19546 起无 else 衰减分支）', () => {\n    const st = mkStore(60, 40);\n    line(st, 8, 12, 30);\n    const { cart, p, game } = mountedCart(st, 9, 12);\n    p.inputX = 1;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 60; t++) cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    const vx = cart.vx;\n    p.inputX = 0;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 30; t++) cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    expect(cart.vx).toBeCloseTo(vx, 5);\n  });\n  it('跳跃（松键重按门）：vy = -5.15、jump = 15；按住续推', () => {\n    const st = mkStore(60, 40);\n    line(st, 8, 12, 30);\n    const { cart, p, game } = mountedCart(st, 9, 12);\n    // 先贴轨稳定\n    for (let t = 0; t < 5; t++) cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    p.inputJump = true;\n    cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    expect(cart.vy).toBeCloseTo(-5.15, 5);\n    expect(cart.jump).toBe(15);\n    cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);   // 按住续推（jump>0 段）\n    expect(cart.vy).toBeCloseTo(-5.15, 5);\n    expect(cart.jump).toBe(14);\n  });\n  it('反向输入：先 runSlowdown 抵消再加速（Player.cs:19567-19573）', () => {\n    const st = mkStore(60, 40);\n    line(st, 8, 12, 30);\n    const { cart, p, game } = mountedCart(st, 9, 12);\n    p.inputX = 1;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 90; t++) cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    const vx = cart.vx;\n    p.inputX = -1;\n    cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    // 反推一帧 = -(runSlowdown 0.2 + accel 0.04)\n    expect(cart.vx).toBeCloseTo(vx - 0.24, 5);\n  });\n  it('加速轨道过车：trackBoost ∓/±4 并入（下帧生效、钳 ±13）', () => {\n    const st = mkStore(60, 40);\n    line(st, 8, 12, 2);\n    seg(st, 10, 12, 30);\n    seg(st, 11, 12, 1);\n    frameTrack(st, 9, 12, false);\n    const { cart, p, game } = mountedCart(st, 8, 12);\n    p.inputX = 1;\n    let sawBoost = false;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 300; t++) {\n      cart.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n      if (cart.trackBoost !== 0) sawBoost = true;\n      if (sawBoost && cart.trackBoost === 0) break;   // 已并入\n    }\n    expect(sawBoost).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\nEOF\nnpx vitest run tests/minecart-track.test.ts 2>&1 | tail -20",
